\"notes\": \"Extended training to 1000 steps CONFIRMS ISR collapse worsens monotonically.\\\\n\\\\n## 500 vs 1000 step comparison (eval_sta.py):\\\\n| Metric | plan001-s42 (500步) | plan016 (1000步) | Change |\\\\n|--------|-------------------|-----------------|--------|\\\\n| eval PR | 0.93 | 0.96 | +3.2% |\\\\n| eval ISR | 0.123 | 0.0027 | -97.8% |\\\\n| proved_only ISR | 0.132 | 0.0028 | -97.9% |\\\\n| components | 6.58 | 18.55 | +182% |\\\\n| ISR=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n| n_necessary=0 ratio | - | 94.8% | - |\\\\n\\\\n## Key findings:\\\\n1. PR improves (+3%) but ISR collapses near-zero (0.0027). The model gets BETTER at proving while becoming WORSE at faithful reasoning.\\\\n2. Component count nearly tripled (6.58→18.55). Model generates longer proofs with more components, but 94.8% have ZERO necessary components.\\\\n3. NOT tautological collapse (comp=2 ratio=0). Instead: contradiction-based collapse — model embeds P(a) and ¬P(a) among many irrelevant premises, making anything provable via explosion.\\\\n4. Windowed ISR trend is monotonically decreasing: 0.1115 (101-200) → 0.0433 (401-500) → 0.0200 (601-700) → 0.0144 (901-1000). No recovery signal.\\\\n5. The exec-only reward creates a pure reward hacking incentive: the model discovers that contradictions + trivially true conclusions always satisfy the prover.\\\\n\\\\n## Implication for paper:\\\\nExtended training is a clean ablation showing exec-only reward's failure mode worsens over time. The 500-step ISR of 0.123 was not a floor — it was a snapshot of ongoing collapse.\"}, \"plan002-additive-b01-s42\": {\"status\": \"DONE\", \"retry_count\": 0, \"created_at\": free)\\n\\n### 决策记录\\n- Qwen3-4B 用 ModelScope 下载（HuggingFace 直连可能慢）\\n- Prover9 从 LADR 源码编译（apt 无包）\\n- 94GB VRAM 单卡跑 Qwen3-4B 完全无压力\\n\\n## GPU 隔离教训\\n\\n### C002: 所有命令必须 `export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N`\\n- 必须在 `bash -c` 内部 export\\n- submit_training_job 命令格式: `bash -c \\\"export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=N && ...\\\"`\\n\\n### kill_job 子进程未清理教训 (2026-05-14)\\n- kill_job 只杀 tmux wrapper PID，不杀 Python 训练子进程\\n- 教训: kill_job 后必须 SSH 验证所有子进程已终止\\n\\n### 磁盘\\n- **C003 约束**: `log_completions=False` + `save_total_limit=1`，output_dir 放 `/root/autodl-tmp/outputs/`\\n- 磁盘爆满事故教训：eval 后立即清理 checkpoint\\n- **当前状态** (2026-05-15): 240G/250G (96%)，Worker A 正在清理\\n\\n## exp1-random-b03-s44 失败教训 (2026-05-15)\\n- 原命令缺少 `bash -c \\\"export CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES=1 && ...\\\"` 包装和 conda 激活\\n- 导致进程启动后立即死亡（无 GPU 隔离、无 Python 环境）\\n- 教训：所有 submit_training_job 命令必须完整包含 GPU 隔离 + conda 激活 + cd 到工作目录\\n\\n## β=0.3 ISR 叙事 (D060/D061, 2026-05-15)\\n- 三 seed 形成 multimodal Goodhart instability：s42 PR 崩塌(18%), s43 中等 gaming(91%, genuine 67%), s44 完全 H7 gaming(98%, genuine 0%)\\n- 每个 seed 走不同的 Goodhart pathway，高剂量 ISR 不产生单一失败模式而是放大跨 seed 不稳定性\\n- plan002-additive-b03-s42 的 eval PR=18% 数据来源：eval_sta.py 在 checkpoint 上运行，但 registry 只记录了训练指标（eval 数据直接写入论文）\\n\", \"total_chars\": 1732, \"truncated\": false}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {}}"}

All 3 seeds exhibit 87-100% conclusion embedding.\\\\n- Hacking taxonomy: 7 distinct modes identified and quantified (plan012 + plan017 D027 + D043 conclusion duplication with ISR inflation + Prolog
